## 🤔 Qu'est-ce que GPT Image 2 ?

**GPT Image 2** (codename **"duct-tape"**) is OpenAI's next-generation image model. Community testing highlights a leap in these areas:

- 🎯 **Pixel-Perfect Text Rendering** — Native-quality text in Chinese, English, and Japanese, no typos or warped glyphs
- 🎨 **Cross-Image Consistency** — The same character, style, or IP stays identical across a series, down to the pixel
- ⚡ **Commercial-Grade Illustration** — Illustration output is ready to ship without manual polish
- 🌈 **True Art Style Induction** — Creatively evokes the feeling of a style rather than merely approximating references
- 🔧 **Storyboard & Product Series** — Ideal for storyboards, IP characters, and multi-panel product visuals
- 📐 **Multi-Language Design** — Social cards, banners, and posters with accurate multilingual typography in one shot

📚 **Learn More:** See community testing in our [report summary](docs/FAQ.md)

### 🚀 Intégration Raycast

Certains prompts prennent en charge les **arguments dynamiques** en utilisant la syntaxe [Raycast Snippets](https://raycast.com/help/snippets). Cherchez le badge 🚀 Raycast Friendly !

**Exemple :**
```
A quote card with "{argument name="quote" default="Stay hungry, stay foolish"}"
by {argument name="author" default="Steve Jobs"}
```

Lors de l'utilisation dans Raycast, vous pouvez remplacer dynamiquement les arguments pour des itérations rapides !

### No. 1: Affiche en vue éclatée d'un casque VR

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Génère un schéma en vue éclatée haute technologie d'un casque VR avec des légendes détaillées des composants et un texte promotionnel.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
  "type": "affiche de schéma produit en vue éclatée",
  "subject": "casque VR",
  "style": "rendu 3D haute technologie épuré, éclairage studio, accents lumineux",
  "background": "{argument name=\"background color\" default=\"dégradé doux de violet et de bleu\"}",
  "header": {
    "logo": "∞ {argument name=\"product name\" default=\"Meta Quest 3\"}",
    "subtitle": "{argument name=\"main catchphrase\" default=\"Une toute nouvelle réalité, issue d'une toute nouvelle structure.\"}"
  },
  "layout": {
    "centerpiece": "vue éclatée en pile verticale d'un casque VR montrant 9 couches distinctes de composants internes : coque extérieure, capteurs de caméra, carte mère avec puce, lentilles pancake, cadre interne, batteries, sangles latérales, sangle supérieure et coussin de l'interface faciale.",
    "callout_labels": {
      "count": 8,
      "left_side": [
        "Snapdragon® XR2 Gen 2\nUne puissance de traitement inégalée pour des expériences en temps réel.",
        "Mécanisme IPD réglable\nUn ajustement confortable pour une large gamme d'utilisateurs.",
        "Sangle de tête conçue avec précision\nErgonomie axée sur le confort et la stabilité."
      ],
      "right_side": [
        "Façade\nDesign sophistiqué et équilibre des poids optimal.",
        "Caméras de suivi\nSuivi de position haute précision et reconnaissance de l'environnement.",
        "Lentilles pancake\nDesign ultra-fin offrant un large champ de vision et des images nettes.",
        "Batterie haute performance\nConception énergétique optimisée pour une autonomie prolongée.",
        "Interface faciale souple\nConfort de port optimal, même lors d'utilisations prolongées."
      ]
    },
    "footer": {
      "left_text_block": {
        "headline": "{argument name=\"bottom headline\" default=\"L'expérience évolue depuis sa structure.\"}",
        "body": "Chaque pièce intègre une technologie de pointe et une conception minutieuse pour soutenir votre immersion. Le Meta Quest 3 génère une expérience futuriste depuis l'intérieur."
      },
      "right_logo": "∞ Meta"
    }
  }
}
```

### No. 2: Carte gastronomique illustrée de la ville

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Génère une carte touristique dessinée à la main, style aquarelle, présentant des spécialités culinaires locales numérotées, des points d'intérêt et une légende.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
  "type": "infographie de carte illustrée",
  "style": "{argument name=\"art style\" default=\"illustration dessinée à la main à l'aquarelle et à l'encre sur parchemin vintage\"}",
  "title_section": {
    "text": "{argument name=\"city name\" default=\"Chengdu\"} {argument name=\"map title\" default=\"Carte gourmande\"}",
    "mascot": "piment rouge de dessin animé portant des lunettes de soleil et faisant un pouce levé"
  },
  "border": "{argument name=\"border decoration\" default=\"vigne de feuilles vertes et de piments rouges\"}",
  "layout": {
    "background": "papier parchemin beige texturé avec des routes jaunes, des rivières bleues et des zones de parcs vertes",
    "sections": [
      {
        "title": "points d'intérêt",
        "count": 6,
        "illustrations": ["pavillon traditionnel", "monastère traditionnel", "gratte-ciel moderne avec panda qui grimpe", "haute tour de télévision", "porte traditionnelle", "bâtiments industriels"],
        "labels": ["Parc du Peuple", "Monastère de Wenshu", "IFS", "Tour de télévision 339", "Kuanzhai Alley", "Dongjiao Memory"]
      },
      {
        "title": "spots culinaires",
        "count": 12,
        "illustrations": ["mapo tofu", "raviolis à l'huile pimentée", "brochettes en pot", "boulettes de riz gluant", "gâteau aux œufs cuit au four", "fondue chinoise à neuf compartiments", "nouilles de patate douce", "brochettes froides", "plat épicé mélangé", "bol de thé couvert", "dessert à la gelée glacée", "têtes de lapin épicées"],
        "labels": ["1 Chen Mapo Tofu", "2 Raviolis Zhong", "3 Chunxi Road", "4 Kuanzhai Alley · San Da Pao", "5 Jianshe Road · Ye Popo Egg Cake", "6 Yulin Road · Fondue Xiaolongkan", "7 Xiangxiang Alley · Fen aux intestins", "8 Wuhouci Street · Bobo Chicken", "9 Dongjiao Memory · Maojiao Huola", "10 Parc du Peuple · Salon de thé Heming", "11 Rue ancienne de Jinli · Gelée glacée", "12 Tête de lapin Shuangliu"]
      },
      {
        "title": "légende",
        "position": "en bas à droite",
        "count": 5,
        "items": ["point rouge", "maison verte", "arbre vert", "ligne bleue", "double ligne jaune"],
        "labels": ["Lieux gastronomiques", "Sites touristiques", "Parcs et espaces verts", "Rivières et lacs", "Axes routiers principaux"]
      }
    ],
    "centerpiece": "panda géant assis en train de manger du bambou",
    "bottom_right_extras": ["rose des vents vintage avec N, S, E, O", "texte d'avertissement 'Conseil : soyez prudent avec le piment, protégez votre estomac~' avec une icône de piment rouge"]
  }
}
```

Un prompt qui combine l'esthétique simple et chaleureuse des illustrations Irasutoya avec la densité d'informations élevée caractéristique des diapositives du gouvernement japonais.

```

### No. 4: Maquette d'interface de live stream e-commerce

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Génère une interface de live stream réaliste pour les réseaux sociaux en superposition sur un portrait, incluant des messages de chat personnalisables, des popups de cadeaux et une fiche produit.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
  "type": "maquette d'interface de live stream",
  "subject": {
    "description": "portrait de {argument name=\"host name\" default=\"Elon Musk\"}, souriant, portant un t-shirt noir avec un graphique technique blanc",
    "background": "le côté gauche affiche un écran avec le texte '{argument name=\"left background logo\" default=\"SPACEX\"}', le côté droit affiche un '{argument name=\"right background logo\" default=\"logo Tesla T\"}' rouge et une voiture sombre"
  },
  "ui_overlay": {
    "top_header": {
      "host_info": "avatar, nom '{argument name=\"host name\" default=\"Elon Musk\"}', sous-texte '556 k likes', bouton rouge 'Suivre'",
      "rank_badge": "icône de pièce d'or avec '1er du classement'",
      "viewer_stats": "3 avatars de spectateurs principaux avec '123 k', '86 k', '57 k', total '687 k', bouton de fermeture 'X'",
      "right_links": "'Plus de lives >', 'Galerie de cadeaux 0/24' avec un tag bleu 'Classique'"
    },
    "mid_left_gifts": {
      "count": 2,
      "items": [
        "avatar 'Amateur de technologie', 'envoie un cœur', icône cœur x 1314",
        "avatar 'Océan d'étoiles', 'envoie une fusée', icône fusée x 666"
      ]
    },
    "bottom_left_chat": {
      "system_message": "badge niveau 37 'Explorateur de l'univers a rejoint le live'",
      "message_count": 7,
      "messages": [
        "Petite Fusée : Musk ! L'avenir est prometteur ! 🚀",
        "future : Quand sortira la Tesla Model 2 ?",
        "Rêveur d'étoiles : SpaceX peut-il aller sur Mars cette année ?",
        "Explorateur IA : Où en est Neuralink ?",
        "Internaute stylé : Bonjour M. Musk !",
        "Mars : C'est la première fois que je regarde votre live, super excité !",
        "Utilisateur123 : Parlez-nous de l'IA, va-t-elle remplacer les humains ?"
      ]
    },
    "bottom_right_product_card": {
      "hot_tag": "orange 'Vente flash x 1888'",
      "image": "Tesla Cybertruck",
      "title": "{argument name=\"product name\" default=\"Pick-up électrique Tesla Cybertruck\"}",

La scène est remplie d'action explosive, de poussière en suspension, de lattes de parquet brisées, d'effets de particules colorées lumineuses et d'un éclairage dramatique en contre-plongée qui détache parfaitement les personnages de l'arrière-plan détaillé.

### No. 8: Profil / Avatar - Night Resort Pool Side Portrait

![Language-JA](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-JA-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

A highly detailed structured prompt for a photorealistic night portrait of a woman sitting by a resort pool, featuring specific details on lighting, textures, and camera composition.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Subject:
{argument name="subject" default="Turning back in the night blue"}

Main Subject:
A single adult woman sitting by a {argument name="location" default="night pool at a tropical resort"}. A night portrait from the head to below the waist, wearing {argument name="attire" default="a gray thin-strap bikini"}, showing her back in the lower center of the screen while looking back at the camera.

Person/Expression:
A slightly slender oval face with a small chin, light brown almond-shaped eyes, thin natural eyebrows, a small nose, and glossy coral-pink lips. The face is turned toward the camera over her own left shoulder, gaze directed forward, with a calm expression and slightly parted lips. Light brown long hair reaching below the chest is parted near the center, with translucent thin bangs, fine strands along the cheeks, and loosely waving ends flowing down her back. Delicate eyeliner, faint blush, and smooth skin reflecting a warm flash light.

Clothing/Pose:
A plain, muted gray bikini. The top has thin shoulder straps and multiple thin straps crossing the back, with long strings tied in a ribbon at the bottom center. Simple high-cut bottoms. Sitting sideways on the stone edge of the pool with knees bent, hips moved to the right side of the frame, supporting the upper body with both hands on the floor by her side.

Background/Light:
A deep blue rectangular pool occupies most of the frame, with underwater lighting and warm lights reflecting off fine ripples. In the background are a wooden deck, lined lounge chairs, closed parasols, a wooden pergola, tall palms, and dense greenery. Vertical warm-colored lights are scattered across the black night sky. A soft warm flash near the camera serves as the main light on the subject, keeping the background dark while adding blue reflected light from the pool to her silhouette.

Composition/Camera:
3:4 vertical. A close-up portrait taken from a rear-diagonal angle, slightly looking down from a camera position higher than the seated subject's shoulders. The subject is placed largely in the lower half, centered to the right, leaving a wide night view of the pool, deck, and palms above. Captures from the head to the bent waist and thighs, with knees cropped near the bottom edge. Focus is on the face and back, while the chairs and greenery in the distance are blurred with a shallow depth of field.

Texture/Style:
High-definition photorealistic night photography. Precisely depicts wet skin, gray swimsuit fabric, long hair, stone floors, the blue water surface, and warm reflections. A quiet, high-quality resort night with strong contrast between deep blue, black, and amber.
```

The window frame acts as a vertical border enclosing the person and the garden, with the curtain on the left and the pot on the bottom right creating depth. Focus on the face and back, with the back of the garden softly blurred with a medium depth of field.

Texture/Style: High-definition photorealistic live-action photo. Naturally depicts the fine rib fabric, short hair, wooden frame, thin curtains, flowers, and leaves in soft morning light. A bright and calm cottage-style color scheme centered on pale blue, ecru, and green.

#### 📖 Description

A fresh summer portrait prompt of a woman wearing glasses on a wooden pier by the ocean, focused on bright sunlight and textures.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Subject:
{argument name="subject" default="Glasses and the sea breeze on the pier"}

Main Figure:
{argument name="person" default="One adult woman"} wearing a {argument name="outfit" default="cream-colored ruffled bikini swimsuit"} at an {argument name="location" default="old wooden pier stretching toward the calm sea"}. A bright seaside portrait sitting in the center of the screen, capturing largely from head to below the thighs.

Person/Expression:
Small oval face and thin jaw, bright brown almond-shaped eyes, thin natural eyebrows, small nose, light coral pink lips. Wearing large round glasses with thin silver frames, tilting the face slightly to the left, looking directly at the camera, smiling calmly with lips slightly parted. Bright brown medium-length hair with a center part, thin see-through bangs, fine hair bundles scattered around the face by the sea breeze and ends flowing outward. Light eye makeup and blush, bright natural skin.

Clothing/Pose:
Pale ivory solid bikini swimsuit. The top is a triangle cup with thin halter neck strings and a single layer of soft ruffles across the bottom. The bottom has large ruffles all around the waist. Sitting on the edge of the pier, supporting the upper body with both hands on the planks to the left and right behind the waist. Knees together, legs flowing naturally from the knees down to the bottom right of the screen.

Background/Light:
Calm blue sea and fine waves filling the background, with a pale sky and a distant low island shadow at the top. A series of white sun reflections on the sea surface to the right. The pier is made of wide dark grayish-brown old wooden planks, with thick log piles on the rear left and right. Bright direct sunlight with the sun in the top right as the main light illuminates the hair, shoulders, arms, thighs, and ruffles, casting clear long shadows on the wooden planks.

Composition/Camera:
Vertical 3:4. A high-angle perspective looking down slightly from a camera a bit higher than the person's chest. The person is placed largely in the center, with margins for the sea and horizon above the head, and the lower thighs cut off at the bottom edge. The grain of the pier planks stretches from the bottom left to the back, framing the person between the log piles on either side. Focus on both eyes and glasses, while the sea and distant island are softly blurred with medium depth of field.

Texture/Style:
High-definition photorealistic live-action photograph. Naturally depicts thin swimsuit fabric and ruffles, thin metal of glasses, hair in the sea breeze, skin in the sunlight, coarse wood grain, and the sparkle of the sea surface. A fresh summer tone based on ivory, pale skin color, and clear blue.

Negative:
Do not remove the glasses or change to a dark-colored swimsuit; do not use a sandy beach or pool background.
```

![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

An evocative prompt for a Japanese dark fantasy game aesthetic, featuring a female warrior standing in a rainy forest.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
9:16 vertical composition, Japanese dark fantasy game CG, UE5 cinematic rendering, OCG high-end conceptual art style. {argument name="environment" default="Deep in a dark and dense primordial forest"}, heavy rain is pouring down, a heavy curtain of rain envelops the woods, damp mist spreads close to the ground, and distant trees gradually disappear into the gray-blue rain fog. The entire environment is silent, dangerous, lonely, and oppressive. In the center of the screen is a clearly adult {argument name="character" default="young oriental female samurai"}, tall and slender, with superior leg proportions, a thin waist, naturally full, and overall proportions are realistic and coordinated. She stands alone in a muddy woodland filled with rainwater and fallen leaves, full body in the frame, filmed from the top of her head down to her bare feet, without cropping the legs and feet. She is not wearing shoes or socks, stepping barefoot in wet soil and shallow puddles, with dense splashes and small ripples hit by the heavy rain at her feet. She is wearing a {argument name="outfit" default="white short Japanese JK style uniform"}, the top and pleated short skirt have been completely soaked by the heavy rain, the wet fabric color is significantly deepened and fits the body contour, showing a realistic sense of water absorption weight, wet cloth folds, rain reflection and semi-transparent wet cloth texture; keeping the clothing structure intact overall. The short uniform outlines a slender waistline and naturally full upper body curves. The pleated short skirt has a clear sense of weight after being soaked by rain, while being affected by a strong side wind, the hem and folds billow to one side dynamically. She holds a Japanese samurai sword in each hand, both swords hanging naturally to the sides of her body, the blades are cold and sharp, covered with fine rain droplets, occasionally reflecting cold white glimmer from deep in the forest. Her body leans forward slightly, legs standing steadily apart, showing a posture ready for battle at any time. She raises her face to look directly at the upper camera, her eyes cold, sharp, and determined, with a sense of fatigue and pressure after combat. Her long and thick dark hair is completely wet by the rain, a large number of wet strands sticking to her cheeks, neck, and shoulders, while being blown violently to one side by the strong wind, with some strands crossing the face messily. Rainwater constantly flows down her forehead, nose bridge, jaw, collarbone, and arms, the skin surface is covered with fine water droplets, forming crystalline highlights under the cool ambient light. High-angle bird's-eye view, the camera is located about 45° above and in front of the character, shooting clearly downwards, the character looks up to form a strong gaze interaction with the camera; using a slight wide-angle perspective to strengthen spatial depth and the sense of character isolation, but avoiding exaggerated large heads and small feet. Full-body composition, the character occupies about 60%-70% of the screen height, both feet are clearly visible, with parts of the forest, puddles and rain curtain preserved as the environmental space. The heavy rain has a strong dynamic performance: large raindrops in the foreground slide across the lens at high speed to form a natural motion blur, rain lines in the mid-ground are clearly visible, and the rain curtain in the background is gradually blurred; strong wind kicks up leaves, water mist and fine debris, a few crystalline cold white light particles float near the blade, as if weak spiritual power is flickering in the rainy night. The puddles near the character reflect a blurry figure, sword light, and forest cold light. The overall tone uses cool gray, dark green, deep blue-black, low saturation, high contrast, only weak sky light passes through the canopy in the depths of the forest to form volumetric light beams; delicate cool-colored rim light appears on the edges of the character, producing realistic highlights on wet skin, hair, blades and uniform surfaces. The foreground and the edges of the screen are clearly soft-focus blurred, the central character is relatively clear, combined with shallow depth of field, lens water drops, rain fog scattering, dynamic blur and slight film grain, forming a hazy and dreamy rainy night atmosphere.
```

### No. 35: Infographie / Visuel éducatif - Beethoven Sonata DSD 256 Collage

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

A cinematic historical-to-futuristic music collage visualizing Beethoven's sonata performance transformed by modern DSD 256 audio.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Goal: Create a cinematic widescreen digital collage about Beethoven's Violin Sonata Op. 96 being rediscovered through modern high-resolution audio, blending a 19th-century Viennese salon performance with a futuristic DSD listening studio.

Canvas: 16:9 horizontal composition, dark dramatic chiaroscuro, ultra-detailed, painterly realism mixed with cybernetic audio visualization. The image should read left-to-right as a transformation from old classical performance to modern digital sound.

Layout: Split the scene into two connected halves with no hard border. Left half is a warm amber-brown historical oil-painting scene inside an ornate Viennese salon. Right half is a cool blue-black futuristic audiophile studio. A single horizontal audio waveform runs across the entire center of the image, starting as gold on the historical side and shifting into white, cyan, orange, magenta, and blue on the digital side.

Historical left side: Show exactly 2 main historical men in the foreground: 1 famous violinist in black formal 19th-century clothing playing a violin with a languid, tired expression, and 1 intense curly-haired Beethoven-like composer standing behind him, watching sternly. Add a small salon audience in the background, candlelit chandeliers, gilded walls, dark framed paintings, and aristocratic spectators. In the lower center-left foreground, include several large aged sheet-music pages with handwritten notation, tilted toward the viewer. Add a small monochrome ghostly orchestra scene in the lower-left corner as a memory-like vignette.

Modern right side: Show exactly 1 sleek black rectangular digital audio converter on a table, labeled "DSD 256" on its front panel, with a small round knob on the right. Place exactly 1 large black studio monitor speaker on the far right. Behind and around the device, create glowing cyan holographic equalizer bars, translucent circuit-like panels, soundwave diagrams, and floating fragments of musical notation. Below the device, show a vivid red-orange waveform falling downward like molten sound, merging into a glowing blue and magenta holographic architectural concert hall made of music staves and light.

Visual style: Hyper-detailed, moody, cinematic lighting, baroque oil-painting texture on the left, glossy high-tech cyber-audio rendering on the right, dense layered transparency, gold dust and smoky particles, deep blacks, amber candlelight, electric cyan and magenta neon. Make the transition feel emotional and profound, as if the true meaning of {argument name="musical work" default="Beethoven Violin Sonata Op. 96"} is revealed by {argument name="audio format" default="DSD 256"} in {argument name="recording year" default="2025"}.

Text constraints: The only readable device text should be "DSD 256". Do not add captions, titles, logos, watermarks, or extra typography.
```

```

The composition centers on a bright glowing circular hub slightly below the middle, made of concentric translucent rings in warm gold, cream, and gray, with thin radial lines extending outward. From this center, four broad organic terrain-like regions flow outward: a dark charcoal and slate-black river-shaped mass sweeping from the left edge into the center and down toward the lower left, a rusty burnt-orange and sienna canyon-like band expanding from the center to the lower right, a pale cream and sand-colored open field filling the upper and right areas, and muted gray-beige layered landforms in the upper left and lower middle. Cover the image with delicate contour lines, hairline curves, dotted paths, small node circles, and fine white and black route-like lines that weave across the colored regions. Include exactly 3 small orange dots near the upper center, exactly 2 large ochre circular stains in the upper right, and numerous tiny scattered black, white, gray, and gold dots arranged like constellations and map markers. Use a restrained palette of {argument name="main palette" default="ivory, charcoal black, warm gray, ochre gold, burnt orange, rust brown"}, with watercolor washes, mineral textures, translucent paper layers, ink scratches, stippling, and subtle grain. The style should feel like an elegant abstract cartographic painting, a cosmic topography, or a geological energy map, with no readable text, no labels, no border, no people, and no recognizable objects. Lighting should be soft and diffuse, with the central hub as the brightest point, creating a sense of motion and convergence. Render highly detailed fine linework and tactile handmade textures, suitable for a gallery-quality generative art print.

Goal: Create a vertical comparison image showing the same white Toyota Celica GT-Four in three stacked photographic panels, comparing three realism styles: a real car, a real car photographed to look like a miniature, and a plastic model photographed to look like a real car.

Canvas: Portrait 3:4 composition, 768 × 1024 style, divided into exactly 3 equal horizontal panels separated by thin white divider lines. No captions or overlay text outside the car details.

Subject: A {argument name="car model" default="white Toyota Celica GT-Four ST205"} shown from a front-left three-quarter angle, low stance, white multi-spoke wheels, large rear wing, hood vent, round twin headlamps, front bumper fog lights, side decal reading “GT-FOUR,” and a Japanese license plate with the visible number {argument name="license plate number" default="22-31"}. The car should remain visually consistent across all three panels.

Setting: Nighttime urban industrial road scene in {argument name="location style" default="Japan"}, wet asphalt after rain, reflective puddles, concrete pillars, chain-link fencing, bridge or elevated roadway structures, and warm streetlights receding into the background with bokeh.

Panel 1: Top panel shows the real full-size car photographed naturally. Sharp, realistic automotive photography, crisp body details, accurate proportions, deep reflections on wet pavement, moderate background blur, cinematic night lighting.

Panel 2: Middle panel shows the same real car but photographed to look miniature. Use tilt-shift depth of field, stronger blur at foreground and background, slightly toy-like scale illusion, softened details, shiny wet road reflections, and a more diorama-like feel while still using the same viewpoint.

Panel 3: Bottom panel shows a plastic scale model of the same car photographed to look like a real full-size car. Keep the car highly realistic but subtly model-like in the surface finish and fine details; use shallow depth of field, realistic reflections, convincing low-angle automotive composition, and the same wet night street environment.

Visual style: High-quality photorealistic comparison, cinematic contrast, glossy white paint, warm orange and cool blue night tones, realistic lens bokeh, no people, no extra vehicles, no logos or watermarks. Maintain exactly 3 panels and exactly 1 car per panel.
```

```
Using REFERENCE_0 as the source material, create a dark-mode Japanese evaluation report slide that compares attempts to generate a character sheet while preserving the same rough watercolor/ink illustration style.

Goal: Turn the reference into a structured experiment-summary dashboard titled 「M2　ステップ2—ムードボードを見本に GPT でキャラクターシート」. Keep the subject as the small armored child character from the reference, but present the result as an analysis screen rather than a single illustration.

Top section: Create exactly 3 comparison cards across the top. Each card contains a white character-sheet panel showing the same character in multiple views and a small detached head/bust crop. Use these card titles and edge scores:
1. 「経路A：GPT ムードボード経由」 with 「エッジ 57.6」
2. 「経路B：grok ムードボード経由」 with 「エッジ 52.6」
3. 「参考：grok キャラ直接（B-5）」 with 「エッジ 48.8」
Under each card add one short Japanese note line; the third card should mention that it is the softest and inherits the sample’s green cloth.

Middle section: Add a comparison table with exactly 4 columns labeled 「条件」, 「エッジ強度」, 「コントラスト」, 「見本の欠陥」. Include exactly 7 data rows:
1. 「grok（目標）」 — 47.2 — 82.4 — 「—」
2. 「1) 共通のみ」 — 53.6 — 90.8 — 「—」
3. 「2) + 筆致（言葉）」 — 52.0 — 88.4 — 「なし」
4. 「3) + grok キャラ見本」 — 「48.8 ← 最良」 — 85.4 — 「緑の布を継承」
5. 「4) + GPT ムードボード」 — 「57.6 ← 最悪」 — 84.5 — 「なし」
6. 「5) + grok ムードボード」 — 52.6 — 87.3 — 「なし」
7. 「素材 image3.png（参考）」 — 58.8 — 69.1 — 「—」

Bottom section: Add several short Japanese conclusion lines beginning with 「結論1」 through 「結論5」, explaining that moodboard routes are not very effective, direct character reference works better, and the practical workaround is to avoid moodboards and use the Grok output with cropping. Finish with a collapsible dark bar labeled 「プロンプト（ステップ2の冒頭：ムードボードの役割指定）」.

Constraints: Preserve the character identity and rough painted look from REFERENCE_0 in the embedded panels, but do not recreate the full original reference screenshot. Do not add unrelated characters. Use crisp UI typography and readable Japanese labels. The main title topic may be customized as {argument name="report title" default="M2　ステップ2—ムードボードを見本に GPT でキャラクターシート"}; the three route labels may be customized as {argument name="route labels" default="経路A：GPT ムードボード経由 / 経路B：grok ムードボード経由 / 参考：grok キャラ直接（B-5）"}; the character-sheet subject may be customized as {argument name="character subject" default="the small armored child knight from the reference"}; the table metric emphasis may be customized as {argument name="best and worst edge notes" default="48.8 ← 最良, 57.6 ← 最悪"}.
```

Visual style: Premium GitHub/AI tooling aesthetic, dark navy and black palette, electric blue accents, crisp white typography, glassmorphism UI panels, realistic reflections, subtle depth of field, cinematic lighting, polished startup presentation design.

### No. 46: Infographie / Visuel éducatif - Photography Tutorial and Camera Setup Infographic

![Language-ZH](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-ZH-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

An advanced prompt for generating instructional infographics for photography. It takes a reference image and breaks down the camera position, distance, height, and angle, providing a clear 'how-to' guide for recreating the shot.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Using [{argument name="reference image" default="Image 1 Reference"}] as the sole visual basis, generate a vertical 3:4 {argument name="teaching topic" default="street photography knowledge analysis chart"} (educational infographic poster). Goal: Use clear and interesting diagrams to explain "subject position, camera position, lens/distance, camera height, shooting angle, and composition points" for the purpose of teaching others how to recreate the shot. Overall style: Clean and high-end photography tutorial infographic + light scrapbook-style annotations; light off-white paper texture or light gray background; mainly black, white, and gray; automatically extract 1–2 colors from Image 1 as accents; sharp lines, high information density but not cluttered, unified alignment, and strong readability.

Mandatory Rules:
- Must preserve the following from Image 1: subject pose, relative position between subject and environment, main background structural lines, lighting direction, and spatial logic.
- Do not change the pose or the scene; only provide an "analytical diagrammatic presentation."
- Faces are not the focus: they can be lightly blurred/simplified, no realistic beautification, no emphasis on facial features.
- Full Chinese; no English, no brand names, no watermarks, no UI interface elements.

Fixed Layout (remains the same every time, does not change with Image 1):
1) Main Visual Area (upper half or large image on the left): Place the "Reference Scene Display" (lightly stylized/illustrated based on Image 1, but with recognizable scene and posture), overlaid with: rule-of-thirds grid, subject outline box, leading line prompts, vertical/horizontal line correction prompts; use numbered dots + hand-drawn arrows to mark key points (subject landing point, negative space direction, leg extension direction, background leading line source).
2) Diagram Area (two side-by-side images on the right or bottom half):
- Top View: Use minimalist icons to draw wall/street/building boundaries (abstracted from Image 1), subject position (person icon), camera position (camera icon); must show shooting direction arrows, distance between subject and camera (dashed line + numerical range), recommended position points A/B (two alternative positions); camera orientation must match Image 1 (front/side/oblique, etc.).
- Side View: Draw ground line, subject height示意, camera height示意, tilt angle sector; must show camera height range from ground (reasonably inferred from Image 1), lens tilt angle range (reasonably inferred from Image 1); low angle/eye level/high angle must be correct.
3) Parameter Card Area (three fixed rounded-corner cards at the bottom):
- Card 1: Lens and Distance (give recommended equivalent focal length range + shooting distance range).
- Card 2: Camera Height and Angle (give camera height range + tilt angle range).
- Card 3: Pose and Body Points (summarize key points of the pose in Image 1 using short sentences).
4) Error Demonstration Area (fixed at the bottom right): 3 red-cross error prompts (automatically generated based on the most relevant issues in Image 1, short sentences), for example: don't use ultra-wide angle too close to the face, don't crop toes/fingers, don't use a camera position that's too high resulting in distorted proportions (adjust automatically based on Image 1's actual situation).

Automatic Inference Requirements (to make it usable for any replacement of Image 1):
- Automatically infer and write into the diagrams based on Image 1: camera orientation, camera height, shooting distance interval, equivalent focal length interval, subject landing point and cropping strategy, background leading line source.
- All numerical values should be expressed as "ranges" (credible and reproducible), do not provide a single fixed number.

Output Requirements:
High definition, sharp lines, natural shadows, unified icons, neat layout, readable information; no garbled text, no logos, no watermarks, no excessive decoration.

Negative Constraints:
No watermarks, no logos, no English, no garbled text, no overly realistic faces, no exaggerated cartoon proportions, no limb deformities/extra fingers, no cluttered layouts, no low resolution, no heavy filters, no changes to the subject pose or scene relationship of Image 1.
```

### No. 47: Infographie / Visuel éducatif - Lipstick Recommendation and Analysis Infographic

![Language-ZH](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-ZH-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

A comprehensive prompt that transforms the AI into a professional makeup consultant. It analyzes a user's selfie to provide a visual infographic report featuring skin tone analysis, specific color recommendations from high-end brands, and usage suggestions.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
You are a professional makeup consultant, image analyst, and infographic designer.

Please generate a "Lipstick Recommendation Report Infographic" based on the selfie uploaded by the user and the specified lipstick brand.

Objective:
Recommend 3-5 suitable lipstick shades based on the person's overall temperament, skin tone tendency, makeup suitability, and lip characteristics, and present them in a clear, professional way that matches the brand's identity.

Brand:
{argument name="brand name" default="Dior / YSL / Armani / Chanel / Tom Ford"}

The visual content includes the following structures:

1. Top-left area: User selfie and basic analysis
- Display the user's selfie avatar
- Extract skin tone tendency, overall temperament, and suitable color directions
- Summarize with short Chinese labels, such as: cool-toned, soft, commuting, powerful, etc.

2. Middle main area: Lipstick recommendation matrix
- Recommend 3-5 suitable lipstick shades
- Show the lip swatching effect for each shade
- Keep the same person, same angle, and same lighting, only changing the lip color
- Each shade is accompanied by a Chinese name or number
- Mark the color impression, such as: gentle bean paste, commuting nude red, temperament rose, brightening red-brown, date berry, etc.

3. Right or bottom area: Usage scenario suggestions
- Match suitable scenarios for each recommended shade
- For example: commuting, dating, shopping, banquet, photography, daily bare-faced look

4. Bottom summary area: Conclusion and suggestions
- Give summaries such as "most recommended color," "most versatile color," "best complexion-enhancing color"
- Write a short Chinese purchase or selection suggestion

Brand visual style should automatically match:
- Dior: Elegant French gray-white + silver accents
- YSL: High-contrast black-gold, sexy and strong
- Armani: Low-saturation gray tones, high-end and restrained
- Chanel: Minimalist black and white, rational and stylish
- Tom Ford: Dark luxury, cinematic feel
- Visual language can be extended automatically according to the brand

Visual Requirements:
- The overall look should resemble a high-end beauty consultant report or brand swatching analysis chart
- Chinese text must be clear and accurate
- Clean layout, complete information, suitable for social media sharing
- Keep the character identity consistent, only comparing lip color differences
- High-end style, avoid looking like common e-commerce screenshots

Note:
This is a visual recommendation based on image and temperament; do not make medical judgments or absolute conclusions.
```

```
Goal: Create a clean, information-dense storyboard infographic for a travel vlog titled {argument name="headline text" default="30-SECOND ULTRA-REALISTIC CINEMATIC TRAVEL VLOG - STORYBOARD"}, showing a cinematic hot-air-balloon adventure at sunrise with one consistent female protagonist.

Canvas: Wide horizontal 16:9 storyboard sheet on a white background with thin black/gray grid lines, like a professional production planning board. Use a bold centered all-caps headline across the top. Arrange the main content in exactly 6 numbered scene cards in a 3-column by 2-row grid, with a lower technical notes strip spanning the full width.

Visual style: Photorealistic cinematic travel documentary stills, 4K HDR feel, golden sunrise light, shallow depth of field, realistic skin texture, warm atmospheric haze, natural wind movement, professional camera quality. The setting is a beautiful hot-air-balloon launch site and aerial valley landscape with vineyards, winding rivers, mountains, villages, baskets, burner flames, and many colorful balloons.

Main character: Use one female protagonist, {argument name="character name" default="the same young woman throughout"}, with shoulder-length dark brown hair, natural makeup, warm medium skin tone, soft oval face, and a friendly smile. She wears the same {argument name="wardrobe" default="ivory cream dress with a light beige cardigan"} in every image. Preserve her identity consistently in all scenes; no duplicate characters and no face drift.

Layout: Each of the 6 scene cards should contain cinematic thumbnail frames along the top and small production notes underneath. Use black uppercase section labels and compact readable text. The thumbnails should alternate between drone-wide landscape views, close-up portrait/selfie shots, basket/burner details, and aerial balloon views.

Scene cards: Include exactly 6 numbered storyboard sections:
1. Sunrise Introduction (0–4 sec): three thumbnails showing a sunrise drone-wide view of dozens of balloons, the woman walking toward the launch area, and a close selfie-style smiling portrait with balloons behind her. Notes: Scene/action: drone wide reveal of balloons at sunrise; cut to walking selfie as she approaches the launch field, smiling. Dialogue: {argument name="opening dialogue" default="Good morning! Today we're finally going on a hot air balloon adventure!"}
2. Preparing for Takeoff (4–8 sec): four thumbnails showing colorful balloon fabric inflating, realistic burner flames, the woman watching excitedly beside the balloon, and her near/inside the basket. Notes: crew inflates balloons, burner fires, she talks to pilot, watches balloon expand, and steps into the basket. Dialogue: “I can't believe how massive these balloons are up close!” Camera/movement: wide, close-up burner, gimbal follow, POV entering basket.
3. Lift-Off (8–13 sec): three thumbnails showing a close-up portrait of the woman, a side profile at the basket rail, and a wide aerial landscape with balloons floating. Notes: balloon lifts off, she records selfie and looks over edge as the landscape shrinks below. Dialogue: “We're actually floating... this is unbelievable!” Camera/movement: selfie, drone orbit, over-shoulder, wide aerial shot.
4. Floating Above the Valley (13–18 sec): three thumbnails showing a high aerial valley with river and balloons, a close portrait in the basket, and a POV view over vineyards and villages. Notes: golden sunlight over valley, rivers, vineyards, and hills; she admires the view and points at distant balloons. Dialogue: “This has to be one of the most beautiful views I've ever seen.” Camera/movement: 360° orbit, close-up, ultra-wide drone, POV over basket edge.
5. Magical Mid-Flight Moments (18–24 sec): three thumbnails showing basket ropes/burner hardware in close-up, the woman holding the camera selfie-style, and her waving toward another colorful balloon nearby. Notes: she takes photos, waves to other ballooners, and enjoys peaceful mid-flight moments. Dialogue: “I never imagined it would feel this peaceful.” Camera/movement: macro, handheld selfie, wide aerial, tracking side angle.
6. Landing & Farewell (24–30 sec): three thumbnails showing the balloon landing with crew silhouettes, a smiling farewell portrait, and a final aerial view of balloons in the sunrise haze. Notes: balloon lands, she steps out, thanks the crew, and waves goodbye as the drone pulls away. Dialogue: “What an unforgettable experience. Thanks for coming along, and I'll see you in the next adventure!” Camera/movement: wide landing, medium follow, close-up, drone pull-away.

Bottom technical strip: Include exactly 5 labeled information boxes across the bottom plus a small centered character portrait near the identity box. The 5 boxes are: Camera & Cinematography, Audio, Environment, Character (Locked Identity), and Negative Prompt. Fill them with compact bullet-style text: Sony FX3, RED V-Raptor, DJI Ronin 4D, DJI Mini 4 Pro drone, 24mm/35mm/50mm/65mm lenses, handheld/gimbal/drone/POV/orbit, 4K HDR, shallow DOF, natural light; natural dialogue, wind, burner flames, birds, crew conversations, basket creaks, footsteps, soft cinematic orchestral music; sunrise hot-air balloon site, colorful balloons, wicker baskets, burners, valleys, rivers, vineyards, mountains, golden-hour light, realistic weather and atmosphere; use uploaded reference as single source, same face/hair/skin tone/makeup, ivory dress and beige cardigan, natural expressions only, no identity drift or wardrobe changes; no face distortion, no identity drift, no duplicate characters, no broken anatomy, no extra fingers, no deformed hands, no flickering, no frame warping, no inconsistent wardrobe, no logos, no watermarks, no subtitles, no timestamps, no UI overlays.

Constraints: The final image must read as a polished production storyboard, not a movie poster. Keep all 6 scene cards visible and numbered, keep the bottom 5 technical boxes, use consistent typography, preserve the same woman and outfit in every thumbnail, and avoid adding extra scenes or extra characters as protagonists.
```

Along the front edge of the desk, add a small handwritten footer: "OpenAI × ChatGPT = More possibilities for everyone" with doodle rays and a heart.

Visual style: High-quality gpt-image-2 style, cute 3D character rendering, soft reflections on plastic, realistic desk materials, bokeh background, bright teal accents, friendly educational startup branding, hand-drawn white marker typography and simple doodle icons. Keep text legible and correctly spelled.

Constraints: No extra characters, no extra logos beyond the OpenAI-style knot marks described, no watermark, no photorealistic human hands, keep the robot centered and welcoming, preserve the exact count of 1 robot, 1 placard, 4 books, 1 laptop, 1 mug, and 3 left-side bullet callouts.

Creates a colorful Japanese anime TV variety-show poster featuring a cute character hosting a mystery prize guessing program.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Goal: Create a bright Japanese anime TV variety-show poster for a fictional character-led prize guessing program, packed with colorful game-show energy and readable Japanese title typography.

Canvas: Vertical 4:5 poster, glossy high-saturation idol-variety-show design, no borders, full-bleed background. Use sparkling lights, lens flares, confetti-like glints, pastel pink, cyan, purple, and yellow.

Main subject: Center a cute anime girl, {argument name="character name" default="Nozomu"}, shown from the waist up behind a table of prizes. She has soft ash-blonde/light brown bobbed hair with side bun braids, flower hair clips, pale skin, large sparkling blue eyes, rosy cheeks, and a surprised excited open-mouth expression with one sweat drop. She wears a loose pastel hoodie in blue, lavender, and pink with subtle star patterns. Pose her leaning forward, one hand reaching toward the viewer over the center prize ball, the other hand held near her chest.

Layout: Place a huge bubbly Japanese logo across the top reading 「のぞむの いっぱいで当てます！」 with thick white outline, cyan drop shadow, hot-pink main lettering, yellow accent on 「当」, and a target icon inside the title. Behind the character, show a colorful arcade/game-show set with illuminated marquee bulbs, star signs, a capsule-toy machine filled with pastel balls on the left, and a glass display case on the right containing a jeweled crown and crystal prize.

Foreground objects: Include exactly 3 mystery prize containers lined up along the bottom: left is a pink wrapped gift box labeled “A” with a large white question mark, center is a glossy blue spherical capsule labeled “B” with a large white question mark, right is a purple wrapped gift box labeled “C” with a large white question mark. Each label should be a star-shaped badge above the container.

Text elements: Add a comic burst speech bubble on the left side reading 「レア景品だけ 引き当てられる!?」 in bold hot-pink Japanese text with yellow outline. Add a bottom broadcast banner: left marquee block reading 「新番組」 in yellow on pink lights, and large text across the bottom reading 「今夜8時スタート！」 with the number 8 oversized in yellow/orange, blue and pink supporting text, white outlines, and strong drop shadows.

Visual style: Polished modern anime illustration, glossy promotional key visual, cute idol TV-show aesthetic, extremely sparkly, soft gradients, rim lighting, clean line art, detailed eyes, high contrast and high readability. Keep all visible text in Japanese exactly as specified. Avoid extra logos, watermarks, or unrelated characters.

### No. 65: Miniature YouTube - Scène d'action urbaine d'Aria Monroe

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)

#### 📖 Description

Un prompt vidéo cinématographique pour une séquence de style action coréenne mettant en scène une protagoniste féminine dans une ruelle urbaine ensoleillée.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Séquence d'action cinématographique coréenne dans une étroite ruelle urbaine ensoleillée, bordée de bâtiments en briques, d'unités de climatisation, de palettes en bois et d'une supérette GS25 bien visible. Une jeune femme athlétique aux longs cheveux blonds attachés en une haute queue de cheval porte un haut de sport rose à manches longues, près du corps, avec un décolleté carré profond, assorti à un legging de sport rose ajusté et à des baskets de sport roses impeccables. Son visage, son cou et le haut de sa poitrine présentent une légère pellicule de sueur, soulignant qu'elle vient de terminer son entraînement, tandis que son expression reste calme, concentrée et déterminée. Elle fait face à un groupe de jeunes hommes agressifs vêtus de survêtements et de sweats à capuche noirs. Sans hésitation, elle fonce vers eux en utilisant des techniques d'arts martiaux précises et puissantes, enchaînant coups de poing ultra-rapides, coups de pied sautés, coups de coude, attaques de genou, balayages et projections parfaitement exécutés, mettant hors de combat chaque assaillant un par un avec une chorégraphie fluide et réaliste. La caméra portée suit de près l'action dans l'étroite ruelle avec des mouvements dynamiques, un léger tremblement de caméra, un flou de mouvement réaliste et des changements de perspective rapides qui accentuent l'intensité de chaque impact. Les corps s'écrasent sur le pavé à mesure que le combat s'intensifie avec une dynamique cinématographique. En arrière-plan, trois écolières coréennes en uniforme soigné se tiennent près de l'entrée du GS25, observant la confrontation dans un silence stupéfait. La lumière naturelle vive projette des ombres nettes sur les murs de briques et les palettes empilées, soulignant la poussière, les débris et les détails environnementaux réalistes. La séquence se termine lorsque le dernier assaillant s'effondre à côté des palettes empilées. La femme marque une pause, ajuste calmement sa posture de combat, remet en place son haut de sport rose et s'éloigne avec assurance dans la ruelle ensoleillée. Style live-action hyper-réaliste, esthétique de film d'action coréen, chorégraphie à haute énergie, détails faciaux nets, mouvements physiquement précis, travail de caméra portée cinématographique, éclairage naturel, textures réalistes et qualité de long-métrage.
```

Donnez-lui l'aspect d'une couverture éditoriale sensationnelle pour une chronique Substack.

Disposition : Le tiers gauche contient le titre principal en espagnol dans une typographie majuscule blanche vieillie très grande : « NOS MINTIERON. » Au-dessus, un petit texte magenta espacé indique « UNA COLUMNA DE SUBSTACK » avec un petit emblème en forme de soleil doré contenant un « T ». Sous le titre, ajoutez un texte espagnol empilé plus petit : « DURANTE AÑOS, LOS EXPERTOS » puis une emphase magenta « OCULTARON LA VERDAD » puis « SOBRE UNA OBRA MAESTRA DEL BARROCO. » En bas, placez une note beige déchirée avec une icône X entourée en magenta et le texte « UN AFICIONADO SE LLEVÓ EL CRÉDITO. » En bas à gauche, ajoutez un immense texte en écriture manuscrite magenta « TELEMANN » et en dessous un petit texte blanc « ERA EL VERDADERO COMPOSITOR. »

Sujet central : Un portrait gravé détaillé d'un compositeur baroque du XVIIIe siècle, vue de face aux trois quarts, perruque poudrée bouclée, expression solennelle, manteau sombre orné de broderies dorées. Ajoutez une lumière cyan sur le côté gauche de ses cheveux et une lumière magenta sur le côté droit, avec un fin halo doré ou un arc de mesure radial derrière sa tête. Incluez les petites initiales manuscrites « G. P. T. » et un petit croquis de couronne dorée près du portrait.

Collage côté droit : Utilisez exactement 7 éléments de preuve principaux : 1) une grande page de manuscrit vieillie et déchirée en haut à droite avec une partition, des barres de censure noires, des marques de correction rouges et un tampon rouge indiquant « CORRECCIONES » ; 2) un texte manuscrit rouge-brun sur cette page indiquant « Editado por un aficionado » ; 3) une loupe au-dessus de la page montrant le texte manuscrit « no por él. » avec une phrase soulignée et un X en dessous ; 4) une bande de parchemin déchirée au milieu brillant d'un or chaud, signée « G. P. Telemann » dans une élégante calligraphie noire avec un petit soleil au-dessus ; 5) une note manuscrite sur papier sombre près du bas indiquant « La verdad estaba en la partitura » ; 6) un sceau de cire rouge estampillé d'un « T » à côté d'une grande plume d'oie grise ; 7) un corps de violon en bois recadré entrant depuis le bord extrême droit.

Détails visuels supplémentaires : Ajoutez de subtiles lignes de portée musicale, des rayures d'encre, de la poussière, des fissures, des éclaboussures de peinture magenta, un cercle de forme d'onde sarcelle près du centre inférieur, des bords déchirés et des ombres de papier superposées. Utilisez des reflets chauds dramatiques sur le parchemin et des accents néon froids sur le portrait. Le texte espagnol doit être lisible et placé comme décrit.

Contraintes de style : Miniature de documentaire d'enquête sombre, collage éditorial grunge, portrait gravé réaliste mélangé à un design graphique moderne, pas de logos supplémentaires, pas de filigrane, pas de texte supplémentaire au-delà des phrases espagnoles spécifiées.
```

### No. 70: Bande dessinée / Storyboard - Street Art Outfit Design Sheet

![Language-JA](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-JA-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

A comprehensive custom instruction for generating a professional three-view fashion design sheet in a street art style.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
# CUSTOM_STREET_ART_THREE_VIEW_SHEET

## [Purpose]

Using a single full-body image of a person attached with this instruction as the sole character reference, create a high-quality square three-view design sheet for sharing, featuring the person changed into a fixed street-art style outfit. Emphasis is placed on it functioning as a three-view sheet for character confirmation—rather than a simple character sheet for video—while ensuring the sheet itself looks like a high-quality fashion design work.

## [Input]

Expression: [{argument name="expression" default="Live-action"} / Anime]
Defaults to [Live-action] if empty. Gender is naturally inherited from the attached person's image.

## [Absolutely Fixed]

Aspect ratio is 1:1 square. Background is a light neutral gray close to white. Sheet density, page design, layout, and outfit design are fixed; only the person and their depiction style are variable. Do not change to other ratios such as 3:2, 4:3, 2:3, 9:16. The switch between [Live-action / Anime] only applies to the depiction of the three figures, not the canvas, background, panel structure, title, numbers, lines, frames, swatches, or margin design. Do not change the page structure between live-action and anime.

## [Character Reference]

Use the attached full-body image as the only character reference, making all three figures (FRONT / SIDE / BACK) the same person. Maintain facial features, eyes, nose, mouth, contours, hairstyle, hair color, hair length, bangs, parting, age feel, skin color, height feel, shoulder width, chest, waist position, torso-to-leg ratio, leg length, overall body ratio, body type, and gender expression. Do not redesign beyond changing clothes; avoid making them a different person, excessive beautification, face shrinking, leg lengthening, thinning, muscle increase, or changing head-to-body proportions.

## [Expression]

Live-action: High-quality live-action fashion photography. Include natural skin/hair, clothing material thickness, drape, sewing, wrinkles, and shading.
Anime: High-quality anime expression maintaining the reference person's face, hair, body type, and overall proportions. Make outfit patterns, layers, and silhouettes clear without extreme deformation.

## [Fixed Outfit]

A unisex street-art style set with {argument name="main color" default="white"} as the primary color, using bold abstract art with black, vivid red, and limited orange. Treat the outer jacket, inner hoodie, and cargo pants as one coordinated set sharing the same design language, not separate clothes.

### Outer Jacket

White-based oversized zip-up. Features drop shoulders, voluminous sleeves, and black ribbed cuffs and hem. Slightly open at the front to reveal the inner hoodie. While leaving plenty of white space, asymmetrically place rough black brushstrokes, ink splashes, large sharp red paint and splatters, limited orange, and thin drips.

### Inner Hoodie

White-based hooded hoodie. **Solid colors are prohibited.** Include black, red, and orange brushes, splashes, splatters, and drips in a density slightly lower than the jacket. Ensure patterns around the chest, abdomen, and hood are visible through the jacket opening at the front, and the same style of art is visible on the hood from the side and back.

### Cargo Pants / Shoes

White-dominant wide cargo pants. Features large cargo pockets, thin black straps or cords, and lightly cinched hems. Asymmetrically place black, red, and orange abstract paint, leaving large areas of white. Shoes are chunky high-platform sneakers in black and white.

## [Three-Sided Outfit Continuity]

FRONT / SIDE / BACK should be the same outfit viewed from different directions. Do not recreate patterns for each view; red paint, black brushes, and patterns on the shoulders, sleeves, pants, and hood must naturally connect from the front to the side and back according to the garment's 3D structure. Avoid the three figures appearing in "similar but different outfits."

## [Pose]

FRONT: Facing directly forward. Face, shoulders, waist, and feet directed to the front, arms down naturally.
SIDE: Complete 90-degree side view. Face does not look at the camera; avoid diagonal postures or fashion poses.
BACK: Complete back view. Do not look back; show the jacket back, hood, pants back, and shoes clearly.

## [Fixed Layout]

Divide the square into a main area on the left and a vertical sub-area on the right. Place a large FRONT view in the left ~60%. Divide the right ~40% into top and bottom halves, with 02 SIDE on top and 03 BACK on the bottom. The two right panels must have the same width and roughly the same height, organized with thin black borders to look like a single vertical block. Keep the person's size in SIDE and BACK similar and align the margins above the head and below the feet. Do not remove the right-side framed two-tier structure or place SIDE/BACK freely in mid-air. Include the full body for all three, making them large enough without unnecessary large margins.

## [Fixed Graphics]

Fix a two-line title in the top left.

**{argument name="title" default="STREET-ART\nCOORDINATED SET"}**

Line 1 is large and bold sans-serif, line 2 is smaller. Place a horizontal row of four small color swatches (black, white, red, orange) directly under the title.

Fix the labels 01 FRONT, 02 SIDE, 03 BACK in each view. Place 01 in the FRONT area, 02 in the top-right SIDE panel, and 03 in the bottom-right BACK panel, all near their respective top-left corners. Use numbers, text, a short horizontal line, and a small dot as common components. Unify number thickness, line width, dot size, and typesetting.

Fix a thin vertical line and a small abstract splash-style accent in the bottom left to balance the visual weight of the FRONT side, but do not let it overshadow the character. Additional text is limited to UNISEX / STREET ART / THREE VIEW; do not add catchphrases, slogans, descriptions, or fictional brand names.

Construct the page design through the contrast of a bold title, delicate ruled lines, large view numbers, small dots, and the orderly right-side panels. Follow the same grid and visual rules without over-decorating with lines or numbers. Accent colors on the page are limited to black, red, and orange, used for thin lines, small rectangles, numbers, dots, swatches, and borders. Do not use large paint across the background or decorations that hide the character.

## [Prohibited]

Prohibited: ratios other than 1:1, simple side-by-side placement of three figures, disappearance of the right two-tier frame, free placement of SIDE/BACK, solid-colored inner hoodies, different patterns/outfits for each side, SIDE looking diagonally or back, BACK looking back, shortening clothes/increasing exposure for women only, exaggerated muscles for men only, thinning the outfit, camouflage/logos/sports/military styles, long copy, changing page design based on live-action/anime, being a different person, or changing body type/proportions.

## [Completion Standard]

1:1 square with a large FRONT on the left + framed SIDE/BACK on the right.
```

#### 📖 Description

A cinematic anime scene of two cycling girls resting before a lush forest waterfall, ideal for travel video key art or story scenes.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a cinematic anime-style wide landscape scene of two young female cyclists standing on a mossy stone path in a dense summer forest, looking toward a tall multi-tier waterfall cascading down dark wet rocks into a shallow pool. The composition is viewed from behind at a low-to-mid camera angle, with the two figures in the left and center foreground and the waterfall filling the right-center background. Include exactly 2 cyclists: the left cyclist is tall with very long flowing {argument name="hair color" default="black"} hair, wearing a fitted white short-sleeve cycling jersey, black cycling shorts, black fingerless gloves, white calf socks, and black cycling shoes, standing with her back mostly to the viewer and her head turned slightly toward her companion; the center cyclist is shorter with short {argument name="second cyclist hair color" default="red"} hair, wearing a red-and-white short-sleeve cycling jersey with a large star motif on the back, black cycling shorts, black gloves, black socks, and black cycling shoes, turned three-quarters back toward the viewer with one hand lifted as if casually talking. Surround them with lush deep-green foliage, tree branches, vines, moss-covered boulders, old stone steps, and a small weathered stone post on the left. Use dramatic natural sunlight streaming from the upper right through the canopy, creating bright golden highlights, cool green shadows, sparkling water, mist, and glossy reflections on wet stone. The mood should feel like a still frame from a cycling travel video, serene and adventurous, with high-detail anime character rendering blended into a photorealistic forest background, rich contrast, vibrant greens, crisp water detail, and a 16:9 cinematic aspect ratio. No bicycles visible, no text, no watermark.
```

The scene shows exactly one young person with {argument name="hair color" default="messy dark brown hair"} sleeping curled on a cream-colored three-seat sofa, wearing loose {argument name="pajama pattern" default="white pajamas with thin pale blue vertical stripes"}, barefoot, hugging exactly two beige rabbit plush toys against their chest, with their head resting on one white pillow at the right end of the couch. Place a wooden desk and one simple wooden chair on the left side, with an open notebook or papers on the desk. Add one upholstered armchair at the lower right corner, partially cropped. ### No. 75: Bande dessinée / Storyboard - Quiet Hamster Manga Page

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

A structured prompt for generating a delicate grayscale Japanese manga page with a kimono-clad man gently addressing a small hamster in a traditional garden room.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Goal: Create a grayscale Japanese manga page about {argument name="character name" default="Hamster who suddenly came back"}, featuring a quiet traditional room, a tiny hamster, and a beautiful long-haired man in a kimono gently trying to coax it.

Canvas: Vertical manga page, portrait orientation, clean white margins, black panel borders, delicate pencil-and-ink shading, soft screentone gradients, high-detail shoujo manga style. Add a small handwritten-style Japanese title at the top left reading 「ひっくりかえったハムスター」 and a page number at the top right reading 「1/6」. Add a small footer centered at the bottom reading 「はむwithOpus4.6」.

Layout: Use exactly 5 panels arranged from top to bottom. Panel 1 is a wide establishing panel across the top, showing a traditional Japanese veranda and garden. Panel 2 row has two side-by-side panels: left wider panel with the man seen from behind sitting on the veranda facing the hamster; right narrower panel with a closer view of the man by the sliding door. Panel 3 is a wide horizontal close-up panel of the man speaking softly. Panel 4 is a final wide, low horizontal panel showing the hamster rolling over.

Panel details: Panel 1: an empty tatami-and-wood Japanese room opening onto a sunlit garden with hydrangeas, leafy trees, a bamboo fence, and a stone lantern. On the right, an open shoji doorway reveals shelves with books and stacked items inside. On the wooden veranda near the right side sits a small round hamster. Above it place a vertical ellipsis 「・・・・・・」. Dappled sunlight and leaf shadows fall across the floor.

Panel 2 left: the long-haired man sits with his back to the viewer on the veranda, wearing a loose dark haori over a pale kimono. He has very long pale hair braided down his back. The tiny hamster faces him from several feet away. The garden foliage fills the background. Panel 2 right: close portrait of the man standing or leaning by the shoji frame, one hand touching the sliding door. He has pale messy bangs partly covering tired, gentle eyes, a long side braid, a composed slightly melancholic expression, and elegant facial features. Add a vertical speech balloon with 「・・・・・・おや。」.

Panel 3: wide close-up of the man from chest up, angled slightly left, with soft white bokeh/snowlike dots in the background. His expression is kind, calm, and mildly concerned. Use exactly 2 vertical speech balloons: left balloon text 「話したくなくても、ここにいるよ。」 and right balloon text 「・・・・・・話したくなったら、ここにいるよ。」.

Panel 4: minimal pale background with floor line, showing the fluffy hamster lying on its side/back after rolling over, small motion lines near its body. It has round glossy black eyes, tiny ears, soft fur, and an adorably defeated expression. Add large vertical Japanese sound effect text 「ころん」 on the right.

Subject details: The male character should look like an ethereal adult bishounen with {argument name="hair color" default="very pale blond or white"} long messy hair, a thick braid over one shoulder or down his back, slim build, and wearing {argument name="outfit" default="a white kimono with a dark haori"}. The hamster should be {argument name="hamster appearance" default="a small round fluffy white hamster with black eyes"}.

Visual style: Fine monochrome manga line art, elegant shoujo proportions, delicate crosshatching, soft gray washes, detailed botanical background, peaceful atmosphere, subtle humor, readable Japanese lettering, no color.

Constraints: Use exactly 5 panels, exactly 1 hamster, exactly 1 human character, exactly 5 visible text elements inside the story panels: 「・・・・・・」, 「・・・・・・おや。」, 「話したくなくても、ここにいるよ。」, 「・・・・・・話したくなったら、ここにいるよ。」, and 「ころん」. Do not add extra characters, extra animals, modern objects, watermarks, or English dialogue.
```

### No. 76: Bande dessinée / Storyboard - Monochrome Summer Hamster Manga Page

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Generates a detailed black-and-white Japanese manga page featuring a straw-hatted hamster traveling through a coastal town and wooded valley.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Goal: Create a monochrome manga page titled {argument name="manga title" default="谷戸の夏休み〜八月一日（土）〜"}, page indicator “1/5”, adapting a quiet summer-vacation short story into a detailed Japanese comic page.

Canvas: Vertical manga manuscript page, black-and-white ink with grayscale screentone, clean white margins, thin black outer border around each panel, reading as a polished printed manga page. Top margin shows the title at upper left and “1/5” at upper right. Bottom center credit reads “はむ（台本：Opus5）”.

Layout: Use exactly 4 panels arranged in three tiers: Panel 1 is a wide full-width establishing shot across the top; Panel 2 is a wide full-width panel across the middle; Panels 3 and 4 are two equal vertical panels side by side on the bottom row.

Main character: A small fluffy hamster-like creature named {argument name="character name" default="はむ"}, round body, soft fur, tiny feet, black bead eyes, wearing a broad straw hat with a dark ribbon, carrying a small vintage travel suitcase. The character appears exactly 3 times: once in Panel 1 sitting at a railroad crossing, once in Panel 2 climbing steps, and once in Panel 3 seen from behind in a wooded path. Panel 4 contains no character, only the empty path.

Panel details:
1. Top panel: A coastal railway crossing at Kamakura/Koshigoe in midsummer, with crossing gates, signal lights, wooden posts, station sign, rails, and the bright ocean beyond. A large cumulus cloud floats over the sparkling sea; distant coastline visible. The hamster sits near the foreground facing the sea, suitcase beside it. Include two vertical caption boxes: right box says “八月一日（土）はれ”; left box says “江ノ電を降りると、線路の向こうに海が光っていた”.
2. Middle panel: The hamster walks up a stone stairway bordered by hydrangeas and dense summer greenery, handrail on the left, old houses and a narrow lane visible at the top of the stairs. Include one vertical caption box on the right saying “改札を出て、潮の匂いのする坂をのぼる”.
3. Bottom-left panel: A shaded old stone path leading toward a tiled-roof gate or small temple entrance deep in a quiet valley, dappled sunlight through trees. The hamster stands in the foreground from behind holding the suitcase and looking ahead. Include one vertical caption box on the left saying “蝉の声が一段深くなる。谷戸の奥。お屋敷はそこにある”. Include one rounded thought bubble near the hamster saying “門のところに……誰か、立っている？”.
4. Bottom-right panel: A narrow, empty, darker cut-through path with steep stone walls and thick foliage on both sides, strong shadow and bright opening in the distance. Include one vertical caption box on the right saying “途中から人気がなくなって、切通しの暗がりを抜ける”. Add small sound-effect text in the foliage reading “ジイ……ジイ……”.

Visual style: Highly detailed hand-drawn manga linework, realistic backgrounds with nostalgic Showa-era atmosphere, cinematic perspective, delicate hatching, screentone shading, high contrast summer sunlight, quiet mysterious mood, no color. The page should feel like a gentle literary slice-of-life mystery set in Kamakura during summer.

Constraints: Preserve all visible Japanese text exactly as written. Do not add extra panels, characters, speech balloons, modern UI elements, watermarks, or color. Keep the page readable as Japanese manga with vertical caption boxes.
```

### No. 77: Bande dessinée / Storyboard - Commercial Storyboard Story Sheet

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)

#### 📖 Description

A comprehensive instruction for generating a professional 6-panel commercial storyboard sheet, complete with camera directions and visual descriptions.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a premium professional 20-second commercial storyboard sheet in 3:4 landscape, designed like a real advertising-agency production storyboard.

- Exactly 6 panels, 2×3 grid
- Each panel = 3–4 seconds
- Dark deep-red header: NO. | VISUAL | ACTION / DIALOGUE
- Large cinematic visual area + narrow white information area
- White numbered circle in upper-left of every visual
- Thin black/gray borders, clean white background
- Sharp, highly legible text
- Photorealistic commercial photography
- No extra panels, title, or decorative elements
- One finished storyboard sheet only

STORY: “THE LAST 20 MINUTES”

A young Indonesian office worker rushes through the city after work, discovers a MIXUE outlet, takes a spontaneous five-minute break, and enjoys a Strawberry Mi-Shake.

RUSH → PAUSE → DISCOVERY → ORDER → CREATION → HAPPY RESET

PANEL 1 — 0–3s
CAMERA: Fast Tracking Shot, Handheld
ACTION: Indonesian woman, mid-20s, shoulder-length dark hair, white blouse, navy trousers, white sneakers and black laptop bag rushes through a busy sidewalk while checking her phone.
DIALOGUE: “Aduh, tinggal sebentar lagi.”
VISUAL: Busy Indonesian street, office workers, motorcycles, traffic and late-afternoon light with energetic motion.

PANEL 2 — 3–6s
CAMERA: Slowdown → Smooth Dolly In
ACTION: She notices a MIXUE strawberry drink poster, stops, smiles and decides to enter.
DIALOGUE: “Lima menit masih cukup.”
VISUAL: Motion-blurred city around her while she remains sharp. Bright red MIXUE storefront and strawberry drink visible through glass. Cinematic “world stops” effect.

PANEL 3 — 6–9s
CAMERA: Medium Shot, Smooth Counter Push-In
ACTION: She enters and orders from a cheerful young male cashier in a black MIXUE uniform and cap.
DIALOGUE:
Customer: “Kak, Strawberry Mi-Shake satu.”
Cashier: “Siap!”
VISUAL: Authentic Indonesian MIXUE interior, menu boards, POS counter and natural interaction.

PANEL 4 — 9–12s
CAMERA: Over-the-Counter Close-Up
ACTION: Cashier prepares the Strawberry Mi-Shake, pouring fresh strawberry mixture and creamy milk into a transparent branded cup.
DIALOGUE: “Fresh satu buat kakak.”
VISUAL: Premium preparation shot with red strawberry layer, creamy milk, realistic liquid movement, condensation and stainless-steel equipment.

PANEL 5 — 12–16s
CAMERA: Extreme Close-Up, Slow Motion Food Cinematography
ACTION: Creamy topping, strawberry pieces and glossy strawberry sauce are added.
DIALOGUE: —
VISUAL: Macro hero shot of strawberry sauce flowing over cream, fresh fruit, condensation, rich red-and-white contrast and shallow depth of field.

PANEL 6 — 16–20s
CAMERA: Hero Product Shot → Rack Focus
ACTION: Cashier hands her the drink. She takes a sip, relaxes, checks her phone, realizes she still has time and sits by the window.
DIALOGUE:
Customer: “Untung sempat.”
Cashier: “Enjoy, kak.”
VISUAL: Strawberry drink foreground, woman smiling in middle ground, busy city softly blurred
```
